id: 5e6c4894-d99a-4957-8d8d-6072fe5c480d
entity_slug: parlor-doughnuts
reviewer_name: Cat HK
rating: 2
body: Visited because of high Google ratings. Nice-looking exterior but by 8:30a.m. parking lot was already full with people waiting by the door.

Luckily they had a drive-thru and we were the only car in line.

Ordered two coffees and three food items then waited for about 10 minutes. Several cars behind us eventually left.

Expected takeout food might not be as good as dining in but we were still disappointed.

Breakfast Taco was supposed to come with two pieces, but we only received one. Sausage and egg biscuit with sweet fig jam tasted odd. Glazed doughnut, recommended by friendly staff, was the only item we enjoyed. Both coffees were average at best.

Not sure what all hype was about. Maybe dine-in experience would be better?
